From handbook product matrix (line 1184-1328):\n    # 8-zone ovens: ~96 inches (244 cm) process area\n    # 10-zone ovens: ~114-148 inches (290-376 cm) process area\n\n    if '8Z' in oven_model:\n        heated_length_cm = 244  # ~96 inches\n    elif '10Z' in oven_model:\n        heated_length_cm = 320  # ~126 inches average\n    else:\n        heated_length_cm = 280  # default estimate\n\n    # Calculate required thermal dwell time\n    # For TAL compliance, need at least TAL_MAX_SECONDS in reflow zone\n    # But the total profile typically needs 3-6 minutes total dwell\n    # Using a typical requirement of 4 minutes (240 seconds) total dwell\n\n    required_dwell_s = 240  # seconds (typical for reflow profile)\n\n    # Minimum speed to achieve dwell time\n    # dwell_time = heated_length / speed\n    # speed = heated_length / dwell_time\n    # Convert to cm/min: speed_cm_min = (heated_length_cm / dwell_s) * 60\n\n    required_min_speed_cm_min = (heated_length_cm / required_dwell_s) * 60\n\n    # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n    meets = actual_speed_cm_min >= required_min_speed_cm_min\n\n    result = {\n        \"run_id\": run_id,\n        \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed_cm_min, 2),\n        \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed_cm_min, 2),\n        \"meets\": meets\n    }\n\n    q4_results.append(result)\n    status = \"PASS\" if meets else \"FAIL\"\n    print(f\"{run_id}: Actual={actual_speed_cm_min:.2f} cm/min, Required>={required_min_speed_cm_min:.2f} - {status}\")\n\n# Sort by run_id\nq4_results = sorted(q4_results, key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\nwith open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(q4_results, f, indent=2)\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal runs analyzed: {len(q4_results)}\")\nprint(f\"Meeting requirements: {sum(1 for r in q4_results if r['meets'])}\")\nprint(f\"Not meeting requirements: {sum(1 for r in q4_results if not r['meets'])}\")\nprint(\"Output saved to: /app/output/q04.json\")\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 5: Best Run Per Board Family\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\nprint(\"QUESTION 5: Best Run Per Board Family\")\nprint(\"=\"*80)\n\n# Select best run based on quality and efficiency\n# Quality metrics: yield, defects\n# Efficiency metrics: speed, downtime\n\nq5_results = []\n\n# Get unique board families\nboard_families = mes_df['board_family'].unique()\n\nfor family in sorted(board_families):\n    family_runs = mes_df[mes_df['board_family'] == family]\n\n    if len(family_runs) == 0:\n        continue\n\n    # Normalize: speed contributes positively, downtime negatively\n        # Max observed speed ~107, downtime up to ~206s\n        speed_score = (speed / 110) * 50  # 0-50 points\n        downtime_penalty = (downtime / 250) * 50  # 0-50 penalty\n        efficiency_score = max(0, speed_score - downtime_penalty)\n\n        # Combined score (70% quality, 30% efficiency)\n        total_score = quality_score * 0.7 + efficiency_score * 0.3\n\n        run_scores.append({\n            'run_id': run_id,\n            'score': total_score,\n            'yield': fp_yield,\n            'speed': speed,\n            'downtime': downtime\n        })\n\n    # Filter to preheat region\n    mask = (temps >= PREHEAT_TEMP_START) & (temps <= PREHEAT_TEMP_END)\n    region_times = times[mask]\n    region_temps = temps[mask]\n\n    if len(region_temps) < 2:\n        return None\n\n    # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n    dt = np.diff(region_times)\n    dtemp = np.diff(region_temps)\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    valid_mask = dt > 0\n    if not np.any(valid_mask):\n        return None\n\n    ramp_rates = dtemp[valid_mask] / dt[valid_mask]\n\n    return float(np.max(ramp_rates))\n\n\ndef calculate_time_above_liquidus(times: np.ndarray, temps: np.ndarray,\n                                   liquidus_temp: float) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) in seconds.\n\n    TAL is the duration the solder joint stays above the liquidus temperature.\n    \"\"\"\n    above = temps > liquidus_temp\n    indices = np.where(above)[0]\n\n    if len(indices) == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    # Get first and last time above liquidus\n    start_time = times[indices[0]]\n    end_time = times[indices[-1]]\n\n    return float(end_time - start_time)\n\n\ndef find_peak_temperature(temps: np.ndarray) -> Optional[float]:\n    \"\"\"Find the peak (maximum) temperature in the profile.\"\"\"\n    if len(temps) == 0:\n        return None\n    return float(np.max(temps))\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 1: Maximum Temperature Ramp Rate During Preheat\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\nprint(\"QUESTION 1: Maximum Temperature Ramp Rate During Preheat\")\nprint(\"=\"*80)\n\nq1_results = {}\nviolating_runs = []\n\n# Get unique runs from thermocouple data\ntc_runs = tc_df['run_id'].unique()\n\nfor run_id in sorted(tc_runs):\n    run_tc_data = tc_df[tc_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n    # Determine compliance\n    if TAL_MIN_SECONDS <= tal_seconds <= TAL_MAX_SECONDS:\n        status = \"compliant\"\n    else:\n        status = \"non-compliant\"\n\n    result = {\n        \"run_id\": run_id,\n        \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n        \"tal_s\": round(tal_seconds, 2),\n        \"required_min_tal_s\": float(TAL_MIN_SECONDS),\n        \"required_max_tal_s\": float(TAL_MAX_SECONDS),\n        \"status\": status\n    }\n\n    q2_results.append(result)\n    print(f\"{run_id}: TAL={tal_seconds:.2f}s (liquidus={liquidus_temp}°C) - {status.upper()}\")\n\nwith open('/app/output/q02.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(q2_results, f, indent=2)\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal runs analyzed: {len(q2_results)}\")\nprint(f\"Compliant: {sum(1 for r in q2_results if r['status'] == 'compliant')}\")\nprint(f\"Non-compliant: {sum(1 for r in q2_results if r['status'] == 'non-compliant')}\")\nprint(\"Output saved to: /app/output/q02.json\")\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature Requirement\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\nprint(\"QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature Requirement\")\nprint(\"=\"*80)\n\nq3_results = {}\nfailing_runs = []\n\n# Check all runs in MES log\nfor _, mes_run in mes_df.iterrows():\n    run_id = mes_run['run_id']\n    liquidus_temp = mes_run['solder_liquidus_c']\n    required_min_peak = liquidus_temp + PEAK_TEMP_ABOVE_LIQUIDUS\n\n    # Get thermocouple data for this run\n    run_tc_data = tc_df[tc_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n    if len(run_tc_data) == 0:\n        # No thermocouple data - run is failing\n        failing_runs.append(run_id)\n        q3_results[run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": None,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n        print(f\"{run_id}: NO DATA - FAIL\")\n        continue\n\n    # Use largest_mass thermocouple as representative\n    largest_mass_tc = run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n    if len(largest_mass_tc) > 0:\n        tc_id = largest_mass_tc['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n        tc_data = largest_mass_tc\n    else:\n        thermocouples = run_tc_data['tc_id'].unique()\n        tc_id = thermocouples[0]\n        tc_data = run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_id'] == tc_id]\n\n    temps = tc_data['temp_c'].values\n    peak_temp = find_peak_temperature(temps)\n\n    q3_results[run_id] = {\n        \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n        \"peak_temp_c\": round(peak_temp, 2) if peak_temp is not None else None,\n        \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n    }\n\n    # Check if failing\n    if peak_temp is None or peak_temp < required_min_peak:\n        failing_runs.append(run_id)\n        print(f\"{run_id}: Peak={peak_temp:.2f if peak_temp else 'N/A'}°C < {required_min_peak:.2f}°C - FAIL\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"{run_id}: Peak={peak_temp:.2f}°C >= {required_min_peak:.2f}°C - PASS\")\n\nq3_output = {\n    \"failing_runs\": sorted(failing_runs),\n    \"min_peak_by_run\": q3_results\n}\n\nwith open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(q3_output, f, indent=2)\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal runs analyzed: {len(q3_results)}\")\nprint(f\"Passing runs: {len(q3_results) - len(failing_runs)}\")\nprint(f\"Failing runs: {len(failing_runs)}\")\nprint(\"Output saved to: /app/output/q03.json\")\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\nprint(\"QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\")\nprint(\"=\"*80)\n\n# From handbook, we need to check thermal dwell time requirements\n# The handbook mentions calculating process speed from dwell time (line 766-767)\n# For TAL compliance, we need sufficient time in the oven\n# The heated chamber length and dwell time determine minimum speed\n\nq4_results = []\n\nfor _, mes_run in mes_df.iterrows():\n    run_id = mes_run['run_id']\n    board_length_cm = mes_run['board_length_cm']\n    actual_speed_cm_min = mes_run['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n    # Get oven model to determine heated length\n    oven_model = mes_run['oven_model']\n\n    # Estimate heated chamber length based on oven model\n    # 8Z = 8 zone, 10Z = 10 zone ovens\n    # Typical zone length ~30-40cm, total heated length varies\n    # From handbook product matrix (line 1184-1328):\n    # 8-zone ovens: ~96 inches (244 cm) process area\n    # 10-zone ovens: ~114-148 inches (290-376 cm) process area\n\n    if '8Z' in oven_model:\n        heated_length_cm = 244  # ~96 inches\n    elif '10Z' in oven_model:\n        heated_length_cm = 320  # ~126 inches average\n    else:\n        heated_length_cm = 280  # default estimate\n\n    # Calculate required thermal dwell time\n    # For TAL compliance, need at least TAL_MAX_SECONDS in reflow zone\n    # But the total profile typically needs 3-6 minutes total dwell\n    # Using a typical requirement of 4 minutes (240 seconds) total dwell\n\n    required_dwell_s = 240  # seconds (typical for reflow profile)\n\n    # Minimum speed to achieve dwell time\n    # dwell_time = heated_length / speed\n    # speed = heated_length / dwell_time\n    # Convert to cm/min: speed_cm_min = (heated_length_cm / dwell_s) * 60\n\n    required_min_speed_cm_min = (heated_length_cm / required_dwell_s) * 60\n\n    # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n    meets = actual_speed_cm_min >= required_min_speed_cm_min\n\n    result = {\n        \"run_id\": run_id,\n        \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed_cm_min, 2),\n        \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed_cm_min, 2),\n        \"meets\": meets\n    }\n\n    q4_results.append(result)\n    status = \"PASS\" if meets else \"FAIL\"\n    print(f\"{run_id}: Actual={actual_speed_cm_min:.2f} cm/min, Required>={required_min_speed_cm_min:.2f} - {status}\")\n\n# Sort by run_id\nq4_results = sorted(q4_results, key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\nwith open('/app/output/q04.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(q4_results, f, indent=2)\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal runs analyzed: {len(q4_results)}\")\nprint(f\"Meeting requirements: {sum(1 for r in q4_results if r['meets'])}\")\nprint(f\"Not meeting requirements: {sum(1 for r in q4_results if not r['meets'])}\")\nprint(\"Output saved to: /app/output/q04.json\")\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# QUESTION 5: Best Run Per Board Family\n# ============================================================================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\nprint(\"QUESTION 5: Best Run Per Board Family\")\nprint(\"=\"*80)\n\n# Select best run based on quality and efficiency\n# Quality metrics: yield, defects\n# Efficiency metrics: speed, downtime\n\nq5_results = []\n\n# Get unique board families\nboard_families = mes_df['board_family'].unique()\n\nfor family in sorted(board_families):\n    family_runs = mes_df[mes_df['board_family'] == family]\n\n    if len(family_runs) == 0:\n        continue\n\n    # Calculate score for each run\n    run_scores = []\n\n    for _, run in family_runs.iterrows():\n        run_id = run['run_id']\n\n        # Get defect data\n        run_defects = defects_df[(defects_df['run_id'] == run_id) &\n                                  (defects_df['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY')]\n\n        if len(run_defects) > 0:\n            fp_yield = run_defects['fp_yield_pct'].iloc[0]\n        else:\n            fp_yield = 0  # No data means poor quality\n\n        # Quality score (0-100 based on yield)\n        quality_score = fp_yield\n\n        # Efficiency score based on speed and downtime\n        # Higher speed is better, lower downtime is better\n        speed = run['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n        downtime = run['downtime_s']\n\n        # Normalize: speed contributes positively, downtime negatively\n        # Max observed speed ~107, downtime up to ~206s\n        speed_score = (speed / 110) * 50  # 0-50 points\n        downtime_penalty = (downtime / 250) * 50  # 0-50 penalty\n        efficiency_score = max(0, speed_score - downtime_penalty)\n\n        # Combined score (70% quality, 30% efficiency)\n        total_score = quality_score * 0.7 + efficiency_score * 0.3\n\n        run_scores.append({\n            'run_id': run_id,\n            'score': total_score,\n            'yield': fp_yield,\n            'speed': speed,\n            'downtime': downtime\n        })\n\n    # Sort by score descending\n    run_scores = sorted(run_scores, key=lambda x: x['score'], reverse=True)\n\n    best_run = run_scores[0]['run_id']\n    runner_ups = [r['run_id'] for r in run_scores[1:]]\n\n    result = {\n        \"board_family\": family,\n        \"best_run_id\": best_run,\n        \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_ups\n    }\n\n    q5_results.append(result)\n\n    print(f\"\\n{family}:\")\n    print(f\"  Best: {best_run} (score={run_scores[0]['score']:.2f}, yield={run_scores[0]['yield']:.2f}%)\")\n    for i, r in enumerate(run_scores[1:], 1):\n        print(f\"  #{i+1}: {r['run_id']} (score={r['score']:.2f}, yield={r['yield']:.2f}%)\")\n\n# Sort by board_family\nq5_results = sorted(q5_results, key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\nwith open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(q5_results, f, indent=2)\n\nprint(f\"\\n\\nTotal board families: {len(q5_results)}\")\nprint(\"Output saved to: /app/output/q05.json\")\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\nprint(\"ALL QUESTIONS COMPLETED\")\nprint(\"=\"*80)\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":269,"oldLines":7,"newStart":269,"newLines":8,"lines":["     #
